- **ssdlc-cysec-comments**: Sweep code (a diff, file, directory, or whole repo) for security-motivated logic that lacks a `[CySec]` annotation and add it, per the ssdlc-cysec convention. Use when asked to add/backfill security annotations, when ssdlc-story-proof flags missing `[CySec]` in the Security Posture / Defense-in-Depth Evidence, or to build the CRA/NIS2 control inventory. PRECISION over recall — annotate only genuine, nameable controls; never invent a reference. ([DogmaSolutions/Kyklos](https://github.com/DogmaSolutions/Kyklos/tree/HEAD/plugins/kyklos/skills/ssdlc-cysec-comments) · ★2)
- **ssdlc-deduplicate**: Perform the global deduplication pass for the ssdlc-story-proof Non-Duplication dimension — find duplicated constants/logic with project-appropriate tooling (mechanical pre-filter), triage candidates SEMANTICALLY, and resolve by centralizing them at the correct architecture level. Use when Non-Duplication is UNPROVEN, when asked to dedup/DRY a codebase, or before closing a story. It is a behaviour-preserving refactor (tests stay green) — and it never merges things that merely look alike.
